- **agent-mcp-trust-pitfalls**: Use when an agent is connected to MCP servers or tools it did not write, or when an MCP server that proxies a third-party API is built or reviewed: which servers to trust and how to pin them (local server trust, tool poisoning, shadowing, rug pull, hidden characters), what a proxy server must not do (confused deputy and per-client consent, token passthrough, SSRF on discovered URLs, state handle hijack, scope minimisation) and the toxic flow (untrusted content, private data and an outbound channel in one session). ([techmefr/mentis](https://github.com/techmefr/mentis/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-mcp-trust-pitfalls))
